This makes it safe to\n26\tshare across threads inside the parallel orchestrator (R-058 / T03.16)\n27\twithout locking. Equality is structural across all four fields via the\n28\tdataclass-generated ``__eq__``.\n29\t\n30\t1. ``eval_id``         — the FR-SCH2-validated eval identifier. They mutate process-local filesystem state (and an internal\n56\tprivate slot tracking the created HOME path) without ever mutating the\n57\tfour DM-006 fields:\n58\t\n59\t* :meth:`setup`                 — ``mkdtemp`` under ``home_root`` and stash\n60\t  the created path on a private ``_home_path`` slot via\n61\t  ``object.__setattr__``. Returns the created path. Idempotency is one-\n62\t  shot per instance: calling :meth:`setup` twice raises ``RuntimeError``\n63\t  rather than silently leaking a directory.\n64\t* :meth:`env`                   — returns the env-var dict the subprocess\n65\t  needs: ``HOME`` always, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` always, and\n66\t  ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when ``time_offset_sec != 0``. The\n67\t  return type pins ``dict[str, str]`` so callers can ``.update()`` it\n68\t  alongside ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` without coercion.\n69\t* :meth:`teardown`              — removes the per-eval HOME when ``keep``\n70\t  is false, leaves it on disk when ``keep`` is true (so post-mortem\n71\t  inspection or NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` tagging in T02.13 can still\n72\t  pick the directory up). Always clears the private path slot so the\n73\t  instance is safe to garbage-collect.\n74\t* :meth:`state_path`            — joins ``suffix`` under the active\n75\t  per-eval HOME so consumers (hook adapter T02.14, atomic setup T02.13,\n76\t  artifact writers) never need to import :mod:`os.path` themselves. The\n77\t  full containment check on ``suffix`` lands in T02.08; this method\n78\t  performs the minimum necessary check that the joined path stays under\n79\t  ``home_path``.\n80\t\n81\tCross-links\n82\t===========\n83\t\n84\t* DM-006 record spec (roadmap row 26).\n85\t* COMP-006 method surface (roadmap rows 28 / 32; T02.07 / T02.11).\n86\t* FR-SCH2 :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05).\n87\t* FR-ISO2 ``containment_guard`` (T02.08) — strengthens the symlink /\n88\t  allowlist / regex checks layered on top of :meth:`setup` and\n89\t  :meth:`state_path`.\n90\t* DOC-OQ8 (T06.03) — gates ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` semantics.\n91\t* COMP-012 probe (T02.05 — ``test_isolation_layers_probe.py``) — pins the\n92\t  upstream ``IsolationLayers`` shape this module sits beside.\n93\t\"\"\"\n94\t\n95\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n96\t\n97\timport shutil\n98\timport tempfile\n99\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n100\tfrom pathlib import Path\n101\t\n102\tfrom .config import EvalConfig, ScratchRootViolation, resolve_scratch_root\n103\tfrom .loader import InvalidEvalId, validate_eval_id\n104\t\n105\t__all__ = [\n106\t    \"HomeContainmentViolation\",\n107\t    \"HomeIsolation\",\n108\t    \"containment_guard\",\n109\t]\n110\t\n111\t\n112\tclass HomeContainmentViolation(Exception):\n113\t    \"\"\"Raised by :func:`containment_guard` when any FR-ISO2 check fails.\n114\t\n115\t    The exception is the single hard-failure surface of FR-ISO2 — it is\n116\t    the only thing :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` may raise once\n117\t    :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` has succeeded and BEFORE the hook adapter\n118\t    (T02.14) writes a single byte under the per-eval HOME. Catching this\n119\t    type at the orchestrator level is sufficient to cover all three\n120\t    containment checks (eval_id regex, scratch-root allowlist, post-\n121\t    mkdtemp symlink resolution); callers MUST NOT branch on the\n122\t    underlying :class:`InvalidEvalId` / :class:`ScratchRootViolation`\n123\t    causes — they are exposed via ``__cause__`` for forensics only.\n124\t\n125\t    The exception MUST raise before any hook deploy or eval-state\n126\t    write so NFR-SEC2 (\"hard refusal before side effects\") is upheld;\n127\t    the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) is responsible for\n128\t    preserving the partial per-eval HOME with a ``setup_failed`` tag\n129\t    once it lands.\n130\t\n131\t    Attributes:\n132\t        check: Short identifier for the failed check (``\"eval_id\"``,\n133\t            ``\"scratch_root_allowlist\"``, ``\"home_path_resolution\"`` or\n134\t            ``\"home_path_escape\"``). Lets reporters bucket failures\n135\t            without parsing the human-readable message.\n136\t        home_path: The per-eval HOME path the guard was asked to\n137\t            validate. Stored verbatim as supplied by the caller (no\n138\t            resolution) so forensics keep the original input.\n139\t        scratch_root: The declared scratch root the guard checked\n140\t            ``home_path`` against. Stored verbatim.\n141\t        eval_id: The eval id under inspection. Stored verbatim\n142\t            (``repr``-rendered) so non-string smuggled scalars are\n143\t            visible in reporter output.\n144\t        detail: Free-form one-line message describing the specific\n145\t            failure. Inherits from the underlying cause when one\n146\t            exists (e.g. the ``ScratchRootViolation`` rendering).\n147\t    \"\"\"\n148\t\n149\t    def __init__(\n150\t        self,\n151\t        *,\n152\t        check: str,\n153\t        home_path: Path,\n154\t        scratch_root: Path,\n155\t        eval_id: object,\n156\t        detail: str,\n157\t    ) -> None:\n158\t        self.check = check\n159\t        self.home_path = home_path\n160\t        self.scratch_root = scratch_root\n161\t        self.eval_id = eval_id\n162\t        self.detail = detail\n163\t        super().__init__(\n164\t            f\"FR-ISO2 containment check {check!r} failed for \"\n165\t            f\"eval_id={eval_id!r} home_path={home_path!s} \"\n166\t            f\"scratch_root={scratch_root!s}: {detail}\"\n167\t        )\n168\t\n169\t\n170\tdef containment_guard(\n171\t    home_path: Path,\n172\t    scratch_root: Path,\n173\t    eval_id: str,\n174\t    *,\n175\t    config: EvalConfig,\n176\t) -> None:\n177\t    \"\"\"FR-ISO2 three-check defense-in-depth guard.\n178\t\n179\t    The guard layers three independent checks on top of the per-eval\n180\t    HOME materialization that :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` just performed.\n181\t    Each check has a distinct attack vector in scope:\n182\t\n183\t    1. **eval_id regex** — re-applies :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05).\n184\t       The loader (T01.07) already validates the id pre-expand and\n185\t       post-expand, but a caller bypassing :class:`SuiteLoader` (e.g.,\n186\t       a programmatic test, a future REPL entry point) could construct\n187\t       a :class:`HomeIsolation` directly. The re-check closes that\n188\t       loader-bypass surface called out in NFR-SEC2.\n189\t    2. **Scratch-root allowlist** — re-applies\n190\t       :func:`resolve_scratch_root` (T01.19) against ``scratch_root``.\n191\t       The helper resolves the path with ``Path.resolve(strict=False)``\n192\t       which collapses symlinks; if ``scratch_root`` is a symlink to a\n193\t       non-allowlisted target (e.g., ``/home/user/.claude``) the\n194\t       resolved form will not match any entry in the resolved\n195\t       :attr:`EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots`. The allowlist is\n196\t       sourced exclusively from the caller-supplied\n197\t       :class:`EvalConfig`; nothing inside this module synthesizes a\n198\t       fallback that could inject the caller's ``scratch_root``\n199\t       into the allowlist after the fact (NFR-SEC2 loader-bypass\n200\t       defense — see D-0029 spec rationale).\n201\t    3. **Post-mkdtemp path containment** — resolves ``home_path`` with\n202\t       ``Path.resolve(strict=True)`` and asserts the resolved form is\n203\t       equal to OR a sub-path of the resolved ``scratch_root``. The\n204\t       ``strict=True`` mode raises :class:`FileNotFoundError` if the\n205\t       per-eval HOME has not been materialized yet, so this is the\n206\t       check that pins \"guard runs AFTER ``mkdtemp``\". Catches the\n207\t       attack vector where a symlinked component inside ``home_path``\n208\t       points outside ``scratch_root`` even though the textual prefix\n209\t       check would have passed.\n210\t\n211\t    On any failure the function raises :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`\n212\t    with the failing check identifier. Underlying causes\n213\t    (:class:`InvalidEvalId`, :class:`ScratchRootViolation`,\n214\t    :class:`FileNotFoundError`) are chained via ``raise ... from`` so\n215\t    forensic tooling can still walk ``__cause__``.\n216\t\n217\t    Args:\n218\t        home_path: Path to the per-eval HOME directory ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n219\t            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. Must exist on disk; the\n220\t            ``strict=True`` resolve enforces this so a caller cannot\n221\t            invoke the guard \"ahead\" of ``mkdtemp``.\n222\t        scratch_root: Scratch root under which ``home_path`` must\n223\t            reside. Need not exist (``strict=False`` resolution); for\n224\t            test ergonomics callers can pass a path that\n225\t            :class:`HomeIsolation` will create via\n226\t            ``mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)`` shortly after.\n227\t        eval_id: The eval identifier the per-eval HOME corresponds to.\n228\t            Re-validated through :func:`validate_eval_id`.\n229\t        config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n230\t            ``allowed_scratch_roots`` is the sole source of truth for\n231\t            the scratch-root allowlist check. The guard refuses to\n232\t            synthesize a fallback config: a missing argument is a\n233\t            programming error, never a \"use the defaults\" cue. This\n234\t            closes the bypass where a caller could otherwise construct\n235\t            ``HomeIsolation(home_root=<attacker-controlled>)`` and have\n236\t            the guard quietly add that root to the allowlist.\n237\t\n238\t    Raises:\n239\t        HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three checks failed. The\n240\t            ``check`` attribute identifies which one; ``detail`` carries\n241\t            the human-readable cause; ``__cause__`` holds the underlying\n242\t            exception when applicable.\n243\t    \"\"\"\n244\t\n245\t    # Check 1: eval_id regex. Loader-bypass defense.\n246\t    try:\n247\t        validate_eval_id(eval_id)\n248\t    except InvalidEvalId as exc:\n249\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n250\t            check=\"eval_id\",\n251\t            home_path=home_path,\n252\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n253\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n254\t            detail=str(exc),\n255\t        ) from exc\n256\t\n257\t    # Check 2: scratch-root allowlist (symlink-aware via resolve_scratch_root).\n258\t    # ``config`` is required: see docstring rationale (NFR-SEC2 bypass close).\n259\t    try:\n260\t        resolved_scratch = resolve_scratch_root(scratch_root, config=config)\n261\t    except ScratchRootViolation as exc:\n262\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n263\t            check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\",\n264\t            home_path=home_path,\n265\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n266\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n267\t            detail=str(exc),\n268\t        ) from exc\n269\t\n270\t    # Check 3: post-mkdtemp symlink resolution + containment under\n271\t    # scratch_root. strict=True asserts the HOME exists (i.e., mkdtemp\n272\t    # already ran). Any symlink inside home_path is collapsed; the\n273\t    # resolved form must equal or live beneath the resolved scratch root.\n274\t    try:\n275\t        resolved_home = home_path.resolve(strict=True)\n276\t    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n277\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n278\t            check=\"home_path_resolution\",\n279\t            home_path=home_path,\n280\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n281\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n282\t            detail=(\n283\t                \"home_path does not exist on disk; FR-ISO2 requires the \"\n284\t                \"guard to run AFTER mkdtemp materialized the per-eval \"\n285\t                \"HOME\"\n286\t            ),\n287\t        ) from exc\n288\t\n289\t    if not (\n290\t        resolved_home == resolved_scratch\n291\t        or resolved_home.is_relative_to(resolved_scratch)\n292\t    ):\n293\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n294\t            check=\"home_path_escape\",\n295\t            home_path=home_path,\n296\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n297\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n298\t            detail=(\n299\t                f\"resolved home_path {resolved_home!s} escapes resolved \"\n300\t                f\"scratch_root {resolved_scratch!s}\"\n301\t            ),\n302\t        )\n303\t\n304\t\n305\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n306\tclass HomeIsolation:\n307\t    \"\"\"Per-eval HOME isolation record + COMP-006 method surface.\n308\t\n309\t    DM-006 fixes the immutable four-field record contract:\n310\t\n311\t    * ``eval_id``        — ``str``, required. Re-validated in\n312\t      ``__post_init__`` via :func:`validate_eval_id` so any unsafe id\n313\t      raises :class:`superclaude.cli.eval.InvalidEvalId` BEFORE any\n314\t      consumer can derive a filesystem path from it.\n315\t    * ``home_root``      — ``pathlib.Path``, required. Scratch root under\n316\t      which the per-eval HOME is created by :meth:`setup`. The dataclass\n317\t      stores it as-is; full containment is FR-ISO2's responsibility\n318\t      (T02.08).\n319\t    * ``session_id``     — ``str``, required. Value :meth:`env` stamps\n320\t      into ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` for the subprocess. Allocation lives in\n321\t      the orchestrator (FR-G2 / T03.16); this record only holds the\n322\t      assigned value.\n323\t    * ``time_offset_sec`` — ``int``, defaults to ``0``. Optional simulated\n324\t      wall-clock offset in whole seconds; :meth:`env` emits\n325\t      ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when non-zero. Full activation is\n326\t      gated on OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03).\n327\t\n328\t    The four declared fields are frozen — mutation raises\n329\t    ``dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError``. A single private slot,\n330\t    ``_home_path``, tracks the post-:meth:`setup` HOME directory; it is\n331\t    written via ``object.__setattr__`` (the documented escape hatch for\n332\t    frozen dataclasses) and never participates in equality or hashing.\n333\t    \"\"\"\n334\t\n335\t    eval_id: str\n336\t    home_root: Path\n337\t    session_id: str\n338\t    time_offset_sec: int = 0\n339\t\n340\t    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n341\t        \"\"\"Re-apply the FR-SCH2 guard and initialize the private path slot.\n342\t\n343\t        The FR-SCH2 re-validation makes loader-bypass impossible:\n344\t        constructing a ``HomeIsolation`` with an unsafe ``eval_id``\n345\t        raises :class:`InvalidEvalId` before any filesystem operation\n346\t        becomes reachable. The private ``_home_path`` slot is set to\n347\t        ``None`` so :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` can raise a clear\n348\t        error when called before :meth:`setup`.\n349\t        \"\"\"\n350\t\n351\t        validate_eval_id(self.eval_id)\n352\t        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n353\t\n354\t    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n355\t    # COMP-006 method surface (FR-ISO1)\n356\t    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n357\t\n358\t    @property\n359\t    def home_path(self) -> Path:\n360\t        \"\"\"Return the per-eval HOME directory created by :meth:`setup`.\n361\t\n362\t        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` when :meth:`setup` has not yet been\n363\t        called or when :meth:`teardown` has already cleared the slot,\n364\t        so the failure mode is \"obvious AttributeError\" instead of a\n365\t        silent ``None`` flowing into an env dict.\n366\t        \"\"\"\n367\t\n368\t        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n385\t\n386\t    def setup(self, *, config: EvalConfig) -> Path:\n387\t        \"\"\"Create the per-eval HOME directory under ``home_root``.\n388\t\n389\t        Uses :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` with the eval_id as prefix so\n390\t        sibling per-eval HOMEs under the same ``home_root`` are\n391\t        guaranteed to be unique by the OS (atomic on POSIX, see\n392\t        ``mkdtemp`` docs). Parallel evals never collide on directory\n393\t        names; mutating one HOME cannot affect siblings because each\n394\t        HOME is its own ``mkdtemp`` root.\n395\t\n396\t        After ``mkdtemp`` succeeds, :func:`containment_guard` (FR-ISO2,\n397\t        T02.08) is applied AFTER the per-eval HOME has been\n398\t        materialized and BEFORE any hook deploy or eval-state write.\n399\t        The guard re-validates ``eval_id`` against the FR-SCH2 regex,\n400\t        re-applies the AC12 scratch-root allowlist, and\n401\t        ``Path.resolve(strict=True)``-checks the freshly created HOME\n402\t        against the scratch root. Any failure surfaces as\n403\t        :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`; the partial per-eval HOME is\n404\t        left on disk so the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can\n405\t        tag it as ``setup_failed`` for forensic inspection.\n406\t\n407\t        Idempotency rule: calling :meth:`setup` twice on the same\n408\t        instance raises :class:`RuntimeError`. This prevents the silent\n409\t        directory leak that would otherwise occur if a caller looped\n410\t        over an instance, and it makes the atomic-setup wrapper\n411\t        (T02.13) easier to reason about.\n412\t\n413\t        Args:\n414\t            config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n415\t                ``allowed_scratch_roots`` provides the scratch-root\n416\t                allowlist :func:`containment_guard` enforces.\n417\t                ``setup`` deliberately refuses to synthesize a\n418\t                fallback that injects ``self.home_root`` into the\n419\t                allowlist — that would defeat the AC12 check, because a\n420\t                caller can construct ``HomeIsolation`` with any\n421\t                ``home_root`` (per-instance, the dataclass cannot\n422\t                meaningfully validate it). Production callers\n423\t                (orchestrator T03.16) pass the run's resolved config;\n424\t                tests build an :class:`EvalConfig` whose allowlist\n425\t                includes the test scratch root.\n426\t\n427\t        Returns:\n428\t            The freshly created per-eval HOME path (also recorded on\n429\t            :attr:`home_path`).\n430\t\n431\t        Raises:\n432\t            RuntimeError: If :meth:`setup` was already called on this\n433\t                instance and :meth:`teardown` has not run since.\n434\t            HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three FR-ISO2 checks\n435\t                failed; the per-eval HOME directory was created and is\n436\t                preserved on disk for inspection.\n437\t        \"\"\"\n438\t\n439\t        if self._home_path is not None:  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n440\t            raise RuntimeError(\n441\t                f\"HomeIsolation.setup() already called for eval_id={self.eval_id!r}\"\n442\t            )\n443\t\n444\t        # Ensure the scratch root exists. ``parents=True, exist_ok=True``\n445\t        # is safe because the FR-ISO2 guard below catches any path that\n446\t        # resolves outside the policy allowlist.\n447\t        self.home_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n448\t\n449\t        home = Path(\n450\t            tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f\"{self.eval_id}-\", dir=str(self.home_root))\n451\t        )\n452\t        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", home)\n453\t\n454\t        # FR-ISO2 defense-in-depth: re-check eval_id, scratch-root\n455\t        # allowlist, and symlink-resolved containment AFTER mkdtemp and\n456\t        # BEFORE the hook adapter (T02.14) writes anything under the\n457\t        # per-eval HOME. Keeping the\n474\t          variable opt-in means OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03) does not have to\n475\t          be resolved before this method is safe to call: a caller that\n476\t          does not request a time offset gets the historic two-key dict.\n477\t\n478\t        Callers ``.update()`` this dict alongside\n479\t        ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` to assemble the full subprocess\n480\t        env; the return type is pinned to ``dict[str, str]`` so the\n481\t        merge is type-clean.\n482\t\n483\t        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` if invoked before :meth:`setup`,\n484\t        because the ``HOME`` value would otherwise have to be a\n485\t        fabricated placeholder.\n486\t        \"\"\"\n487\t\n488\t        env: dict[str, str] = {\n489\t            \"HOME\": str(self.home_path),\n490\t            \"CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\": self.session_id,\n491\t        }\n492\t        if self.time_offset_sec != 0:\n493\t            env[\"CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET\"] = str(self.time_offset_sec)\n494\t        return env\n495\t\n496\t    def teardown(self, keep: bool) -> None:\n497\t        \"\"\"Tear down the per-eval HOME directory.\n498\t\n499\t        * ``keep=False`` removes the directory tree (``shutil.rmtree``\n500\t          with ``ignore_errors=False``; any failure surfaces to the\n501\t          caller so the orchestrator can decide whether to flag the\n502\t          eval as cleanup-failed).\n503\t        * ``keep=True`` leaves the directory on disk for post-mortem\n504\t          inspection — this is the branch the NFR-ISO2 atomic setup\n505\t          wrapper (T02.13) takes when ``setup`` partially fails, and the\n506\t          branch :meth:`teardown` is called on under ``--keep-home``\n507\t          (T03.18).\n508\t\n509\t        Either way, the private ``_home_path`` slot is cleared after\n510\t        teardown, so subsequent :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` calls\n511\t        fail loudly instead of pointing at a stale (possibly deleted)\n512\t        path. Calling :meth:`teardown` when no HOME has been set up is\n513\t        a no-op — callers are not required to track setup state\n514\t        themselves.\n515\t        \"\"\"\n516\t\n517\t        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n518\t        if home is None:\n519\t            return  # No-op: setup never ran, or teardown already ran.\n520\t\n521\t        try:\n522\t            if not keep:\n523\t                shutil.rmtree(home)\n524\t        finally:\n525\t            # Always clear the slot — even if rmtree raised, the path\n526\t            # is no longer trustworthy for future env() / state_path()\n527\t            # consumers and callers will re-run setup() if they want a\n528\t            # fresh HOME.\n529\t            object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n530\t\n531\t    def state_path(self, suffix: str) -> Path:\n532\t        \"\"\"Return ``home_path / suffix`` after a light containment check.\n533\t\n534\t        ``suffix`` must be a relative path (no leading separator) and\n535\t        must not contain ``..`` components — otherwise the returned\n536\t        path could escape ``home_path`` and undermine the\n537\t        per-eval HOME isolation. Full FR-ISO2 containment\n538\t        (symlink resolution + allowlist) lands in T02.08; this method\n539\t        performs only the minimum check necessary to keep the\n540\t        primitive safe in isolation.\n541\t\n542\t        Raises :class:`ValueError` for any suffix that escapes\n543\t        ``home_path``. The COMP-012 probe at\n``tests/cli/eval/test_isolation_layers_probe.py`` (Task T02.05) must keep\npassing after this extension, which it does because nothing in\n``cli/sprint/executor.py`` is touched.\n\nDM-006 record (T02.04) — the four immutable fields\n====================================================\n\n``HomeIsolation`` is a ``@dataclass(frozen=True)`` so once an instance is\nbuilt it cannot be mutated by downstream consumers. This makes it safe to\nshare across threads inside the parallel orchestrator (R-058 / T03.16)\nwithout locking. Equality is structural across all four fields via the\ndataclass-generated ``__eq__``.\n\n1. ``eval_id``         — the FR-SCH2-validated eval identifier. The id\n   flows directly into the per-eval HOME by :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`\n   so it MUST satisfy the FR-SCH2 regex before any filesystem write. The\n   ``__post_init__`` hook re-applies :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05) so\n   a ``HomeIsolation`` can never be constructed with an unsafe id even\n   when a caller bypasses the loader.\n2. ``home_root``       — the scratch root directory under which the\n   per-eval HOME is ``mkdtemp``-ed by :meth:`setup`. Full FR-ISO2 path\n   containment lands in T02.08; this module performs only the minimal\n   ``parents=True, exist_ok=True`` materialization needed to create\n   sibling per-eval HOMEs concurrently.\n3. ``session_id``      — the value :meth:`env` stamps into\n   ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID``. Allocation lives in the orchestrator (FR-G2 /\n   T03.16); this record only holds the assigned value.\n4. ``time_offset_sec`` — optional offset in whole seconds for the eval's\n   simulated wall clock. Default ``0`` matches DM-006 verbatim; when the\n   value is non-zero :meth:`env` adds ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET``. Whether\n   the subprocess actually honors the variable is gated on OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8\n   / T06.03); keeping the offset opt-in via non-zero values means the\n   env dict stays minimal until OQ-8 resolves.\n\nCOMP-006 method surface (T02.07) — FR-ISO1\n==========================================\n\nThe four methods below are the externally observable surface of\nCOMP-006. They mutate process-local filesystem state (and an internal\nprivate slot tracking the created HOME path) without ever mutating the\nfour DM-006 fields:\n\n* :meth:`setup`                 — ``mkdtemp`` under ``home_root`` and stash\n  the created path on a private ``_home_path`` slot via\n  ``object.__setattr__``. Returns the created path. Idempotency is one-\n  shot per instance: calling :meth:`setup` twice raises ``RuntimeError``\n  rather than silently leaking a directory.\n* :meth:`env`                   — returns the env-var dict the subprocess\n  needs: ``HOME`` always, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` always, and\n  ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when ``time_offset_sec != 0``. The\n  return type pins ``dict[str, str]`` so callers can ``.update()`` it\n  alongside ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` without coercion.\n* :meth:`teardown`              — removes the per-eval HOME when ``keep``\n  is false, leaves it on disk when ``keep`` is true (so post-mortem\n  inspection or NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` tagging in T02.13 can still\n  pick the directory up). Always clears the private path slot so the\n  instance is safe to garbage-collect.\n* :meth:`state_path`            — joins ``suffix`` under the active\n  per-eval HOME so consumers (hook adapter T02.14, atomic setup T02.13,\n  artifact writers) never need to import :mod:`os.path` themselves. The\n  full containment check on ``suffix`` lands in T02.08; this method\n  performs the minimum necessary check that the joined path stays under\n  ``home_path``.\n\nCross-links\n===========\n\n* DM-006 record spec (roadmap row 26).\n* COMP-006 method surface (roadmap rows 28 / 32; T02.07 / T02.11).\n* FR-SCH2 :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05).\n* FR-ISO2 ``containment_guard`` (T02.08) — strengthens the symlink /\n  allowlist / regex checks layered on top of :meth:`setup` and\n  :meth:`state_path`.\n* DOC-OQ8 (T06.03) — gates ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` semantics.\n* COMP-012 probe (T02.05 — ``test_isolation_layers_probe.py``) — pins the\n  upstream ``IsolationLayers`` shape this module sits beside.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nimport tempfile\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom .config import EvalConfig, ScratchRootViolation, resolve_scratch_root\nfrom .loader import InvalidEvalId, validate_eval_id\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"HomeContainmentViolation\",\n    \"HomeIsolation\",\n    \"containment_guard\",\n]\n\n\nclass HomeContainmentViolation(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :func:`containment_guard` when any FR-ISO2 check fails.\n\n    The exception is the single hard-failure surface of FR-ISO2 — it is\n    the only thing :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` may raise once\n    :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` has succeeded and BEFORE the hook adapter\n    (T02.14) writes a single byte under the per-eval HOME. Catching this\n    type at the orchestrator level is sufficient to cover all three\n    containment checks (eval_id regex, scratch-root allowlist, post-\n    mkdtemp symlink resolution); callers MUST NOT branch on the\n    underlying :class:`InvalidEvalId` / :class:`ScratchRootViolation`\n    causes — they are exposed via ``__cause__`` for forensics only.\n\n    The exception MUST raise before any hook deploy or eval-state\n    write so NFR-SEC2 (\"hard refusal before side effects\") is upheld;\n    the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) is responsible for\n    preserving the partial per-eval HOME with a ``setup_failed`` tag\n    once it lands.\n\n    Attributes:\n        check: Short identifier for the failed check (``\"eval_id\"``,\n            ``\"scratch_root_allowlist\"``, ``\"home_path_resolution\"`` or\n            ``\"home_path_escape\"``). Lets reporters bucket failures\n            without parsing the human-readable message.\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME path the guard was asked to\n            validate. Stored verbatim as supplied by the caller (no\n            resolution) so forensics keep the original input.\n        scratch_root: The declared scratch root the guard checked\n            ``home_path`` against. Stored verbatim.\n        eval_id: The eval id under inspection. Stored verbatim\n            (``repr``-rendered) so non-string smuggled scalars are\n            visible in reporter output.\n        detail: Free-form one-line message describing the specific\n            failure. Inherits from the underlying cause when one\n            exists (e.g. the ``ScratchRootViolation`` rendering).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        check: str,\n        home_path: Path,\n        scratch_root: Path,\n        eval_id: object,\n        detail: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.check = check\n        self.home_path = home_path\n        self.scratch_root = scratch_root\n        self.eval_id = eval_id\n        self.detail = detail\n        super().__init__(\n            f\"FR-ISO2 containment check {check!r} failed for \"\n            f\"eval_id={eval_id!r} home_path={home_path!s} \"\n            f\"scratch_root={scratch_root!s}: {detail}\"\n        )\n\n\ndef containment_guard(\n    home_path: Path,\n    scratch_root: Path,\n    eval_id: str,\n    *,\n    config: EvalConfig,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"FR-ISO2 three-check defense-in-depth guard.\n\n    The guard layers three independent checks on top of the per-eval\n    HOME materialization that :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` just performed.\n    Each check has a distinct attack vector in scope:\n\n    1. **eval_id regex** — re-applies :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05).\n       The loader (T01.07) already validates the id pre-expand and\n       post-expand, but a caller bypassing :class:`SuiteLoader` (e.g.,\n       a programmatic test, a future REPL entry point) could construct\n       a :class:`HomeIsolation` directly. The re-check closes that\n       loader-bypass surface called out in NFR-SEC2.\n    2. **Scratch-root allowlist** — re-applies\n       :func:`resolve_scratch_root` (T01.19) against ``scratch_root``.\n       The helper resolves the path with ``Path.resolve(strict=False)``\n       which collapses symlinks; if ``scratch_root`` is a symlink to a\n       non-allowlisted target (e.g., ``/home/user/.claude``) the\n       resolved form will not match any entry in the resolved\n       :attr:`EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots`. The allowlist is\n       sourced exclusively from the caller-supplied\n       :class:`EvalConfig`; nothing inside this module synthesizes a\n       fallback that could inject the caller's ``scratch_root``\n       into the allowlist after the fact (NFR-SEC2 loader-bypass\n       defense — see D-0029 spec rationale).\n    3. **Post-mkdtemp path containment** — resolves ``home_path`` with\n       ``Path.resolve(strict=True)`` and asserts the resolved form is\n       equal to OR a sub-path of the resolved ``scratch_root``. The\n       ``strict=True`` mode raises :class:`FileNotFoundError` if the\n       per-eval HOME has not been materialized yet, so this is the\n       check that pins \"guard runs AFTER ``mkdtemp``\". Catches the\n       attack vector where a symlinked component inside ``home_path``\n       points outside ``scratch_root`` even though the textual prefix\n       check would have passed.\n\n    On any failure the function raises :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`\n    with the failing check identifier. Underlying causes\n    (:class:`InvalidEvalId`, :class:`ScratchRootViolation`,\n    :class:`FileNotFoundError`) are chained via ``raise ... from`` so\n    forensic tooling can still walk ``__cause__``.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: Path to the per-eval HOME directory ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. Must exist on disk; the\n            ``strict=True`` resolve enforces this so a caller cannot\n            invoke the guard \"ahead\" of ``mkdtemp``.\n        scratch_root: Scratch root under which ``home_path`` must\n            reside. Need not exist (``strict=False`` resolution); for\n            test ergonomics callers can pass a path that\n            :class:`HomeIsolation` will create via\n            ``mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)`` shortly after.\n        eval_id: The eval identifier the per-eval HOME corresponds to.\n            Re-validated through :func:`validate_eval_id`.\n        config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n            ``allowed_scratch_roots`` is the sole source of truth for\n            the scratch-root allowlist check. The guard refuses to\n            synthesize a fallback config: a missing argument is a\n            programming error, never a \"use the defaults\" cue. This\n            closes the bypass where a caller could otherwise construct\n            ``HomeIsolation(home_root=<attacker-controlled>)`` and have\n            the guard quietly add that root to the allowlist.\n\n    Raises:\n        HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three checks failed. The\n            ``check`` attribute identifies which one; ``detail`` carries\n            the human-readable cause; ``__cause__`` holds the underlying\n            exception when applicable.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Check 1: eval_id regex. Loader-bypass defense.\n    try:\n        validate_eval_id(eval_id)\n    except InvalidEvalId as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"eval_id\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=str(exc),\n        ) from exc\n\n    # Check 2: scratch-root allowlist (symlink-aware via resolve_scratch_root).\n    # ``config`` is required: see docstring rationale (NFR-SEC2 bypass close).\n    try:\n        resolved_scratch = resolve_scratch_root(scratch_root, config=config)\n    except ScratchRootViolation as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=str(exc),\n        ) from exc\n\n    # Check 3: post-mkdtemp symlink resolution + containment under\n    # scratch_root. strict=True asserts the HOME exists (i.e., mkdtemp\n    # already ran). Any symlink inside home_path is collapsed; the\n    # resolved form must equal or live beneath the resolved scratch root.\n    try:\n        resolved_home = home_path.resolve(strict=True)\n    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"home_path_resolution\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=(\n                \"home_path does not exist on disk; FR-ISO2 requires the \"\n                \"guard to run AFTER mkdtemp materialized the per-eval \"\n                \"HOME\"\n            ),\n        ) from exc\n\n    if not (\n        resolved_home == resolved_scratch\n        or resolved_home.is_relative_to(resolved_scratch)\n    ):\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"home_path_escape\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=(\n                f\"resolved home_path {resolved_home!s} escapes resolved \"\n                f\"scratch_root {resolved_scratch!s}\"\n            ),\n        )\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass HomeIsolation:\n    \"\"\"Per-eval HOME isolation record + COMP-006 method surface.\n\n    DM-006 fixes the immutable four-field record contract:\n\n    * ``eval_id``        — ``str``, required. Re-validated in\n      ``__post_init__`` via :func:`validate_eval_id` so any unsafe id\n      raises :class:`superclaude.cli.eval.InvalidEvalId` BEFORE any\n      consumer can derive a filesystem path from it.\n    * ``home_root``      — ``pathlib.Path``, required. Scratch root under\n      which the per-eval HOME is created by :meth:`setup`. The dataclass\n      stores it as-is; full containment is FR-ISO2's responsibility\n      (T02.08).\n    * ``session_id``     — ``str``, required. Value :meth:`env` stamps\n      into ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` for the subprocess. Allocation lives in\n      the orchestrator (FR-G2 / T03.16); this record only holds the\n      assigned value.\n    * ``time_offset_sec`` — ``int``, defaults to ``0``. Optional simulated\n      wall-clock offset in whole seconds; :meth:`env` emits\n      ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when non-zero. Full activation is\n      gated on OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03).\n\n    The four declared fields are frozen — mutation raises\n    ``dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError``. A single private slot,\n    ``_home_path``, tracks the post-:meth:`setup` HOME directory; it is\n    written via ``object.__setattr__`` (the documented escape hatch for\n    frozen dataclasses) and never participates in equality or hashing.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    eval_id: str\n    home_root: Path\n    session_id: str\n    time_offset_sec: int = 0\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Re-apply the FR-SCH2 guard and initialize the private path slot.\n\n        The FR-SCH2 re-validation makes loader-bypass impossible:\n        constructing a ``HomeIsolation`` with an unsafe ``eval_id``\n        raises :class:`InvalidEvalId` before any filesystem operation\n        becomes reachable. The private ``_home_path`` slot is set to\n        ``None`` so :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` can raise a clear\n        error when called before :meth:`setup`.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        validate_eval_id(self.eval_id)\n        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # COMP-006 method surface (FR-ISO1)\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def home_path(self) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Return the per-eval HOME directory created by :meth:`setup`.\n\n        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` when :meth:`setup` has not yet been\n        called or when :meth:`teardown` has already cleared the slot,\n        so the failure mode is \"obvious AttributeError\" instead of a\n        silent ``None`` flowing into an env dict.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n        if home is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                \"HomeIsolation.setup() must be called before accessing home_path\"\n            )\n        return home\n\n    @property\n    def is_set_up(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Return ``True`` when :meth:`setup` has created a per-eval HOME\n        and :meth:`teardown` has not yet been called.\n\n        Lets callers (atomic setup wrapper T02.13, the orchestrator)\n        ask whether teardown is needed without catching exceptions.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        return self._home_path is not None  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n\n    def setup(self, *, config: EvalConfig) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Create the per-eval HOME directory under ``home_root``.\n\n        Uses :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` with the eval_id as prefix so\n        sibling per-eval HOMEs under the same ``home_root`` are\n        guaranteed to be unique by the OS (atomic on POSIX, see\n        ``mkdtemp`` docs). Parallel evals never collide on directory\n        names; mutating one HOME cannot affect siblings because each\n        HOME is its own ``mkdtemp`` root.\n\n        After ``mkdtemp`` succeeds, :func:`containment_guard` (FR-ISO2,\n        T02.08) is applied AFTER the per-eval HOME has been\n        materialized and BEFORE any hook deploy or eval-state write.\n        The guard re-validates ``eval_id`` against the FR-SCH2 regex,\n        re-applies the AC12 scratch-root allowlist, and\n        ``Path.resolve(strict=True)``-checks the freshly created HOME\n        against the scratch root. Any failure surfaces as\n        :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`; the partial per-eval HOME is\n        left on disk so the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can\n        tag it as ``setup_failed`` for forensic inspection.\n\n        Idempotency rule: calling :meth:`setup` twice on the same\n        instance raises :class:`RuntimeError`. This prevents the silent\n        directory leak that would otherwise occur if a caller looped\n        over an instance, and it makes the atomic-setup wrapper\n        (T02.13) easier to reason about.\n\n        Args:\n            config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n                ``allowed_scratch_roots`` provides the scratch-root\n                allowlist :func:`containment_guard` enforces.\n                ``setup`` deliberately refuses to synthesize a\n                fallback that injects ``self.home_root`` into the\n                allowlist — that would defeat the AC12 check, because a\n                caller can construct ``HomeIsolation`` with any\n                ``home_root`` (per-instance, the dataclass cannot\n                meaningfully validate it). Production callers\n                (orchestrator T03.16) pass the run's resolved config;\n                tests build an :class:`EvalConfig` whose allowlist\n                includes the test scratch root.\n\n        Returns:\n            The freshly created per-eval HOME path (also recorded on\n            :attr:`home_path`).\n\n        Raises:\n            RuntimeError: If :meth:`setup` was already called on this\n                instance and :meth:`teardown` has not run since.\n            HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three FR-ISO2 checks\n                failed; the per-eval HOME directory was created and is\n                preserved on disk for inspection.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if self._home_path is not None:  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                f\"HomeIsolation.setup() already called for eval_id={self.eval_id!r}\"\n            )\n\n        # Ensure the scratch root exists. ``parents=True, exist_ok=True``\n        # is safe because the FR-ISO2 guard below catches any path that\n        # resolves outside the policy allowlist.\n        self.home_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        home = Path(\n            tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f\"{self.eval_id}-\", dir=str(self.home_root))\n        )\n        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", home)\n\n        # FR-ISO2 defense-in-depth: re-check eval_id, scratch-root\n        # allowlist, and symlink-resolved containment AFTER mkdtemp and\n        # BEFORE the hook adapter (T02.14) writes anything under the\n        # per-eval HOME. A failure here leaves the directory on disk so\n        # the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can tag it.\n        containment_guard(\n            home_path=home,\n            scratch_root=self.home_root,\n            eval_id=self.eval_id,\n            config=config,\n        )\n        return home\n\n    def env(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the env-var dict the subprocess needs for HOME isolation.\n\n        * ``HOME`` is always present.\n        * ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` is always present (FR-ISO1 mandate).\n        * ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` is present only when\n          ``time_offset_sec`` differs from the default ``0``. Keeping the\n          variable opt-in means OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03) does not have to\n          be resolved before this method is safe to call: a caller that\n          does not request a time offset gets the historic two-key dict.\n\n        Callers ``.update()`` this dict alongside\n        ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` to assemble the full subprocess\n        env; the return type is pinned to ``dict[str, str]`` so the\n        merge is type-clean.\n\n        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` if invoked before :meth:`setup`,\n        because the ``HOME`` value would otherwise have to be a\n        fabricated placeholder.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        env: dict[str, str] = {\n            \"HOME\": str(self.home_path),\n            \"CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\": self.session_id,\n        }\n        if self.time_offset_sec != 0:\n            env[\"CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET\"] = str(self.time_offset_sec)\n        return env\n\n    def teardown(self, keep: bool) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Tear down the per-eval HOME directory.\n\n        * ``keep=False`` removes the directory tree (``shutil.rmtree``\n          with ``ignore_errors=False``; any failure surfaces to the\n          caller so the orchestrator can decide whether to flag the\n          eval as cleanup-failed).\n        * ``keep=True`` leaves the directory on disk for post-mortem\n          inspection — this is the branch the NFR-ISO2 atomic setup\n          wrapper (T02.13) takes when ``setup`` partially fails, and the\n          branch :meth:`teardown` is called on under ``--keep-home``\n          (T03.18).\n\n        Either way, the private ``_home_path`` slot is cleared after\n        teardown, so subsequent :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` calls\n        fail loudly instead of pointing at a stale (possibly deleted)\n        path. Calling :meth:`teardown` when no HOME has been set up is\n        a no-op — callers are not required to track setup state\n        themselves.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n        if home is None:\n            return  # No-op: setup never ran, or teardown already ran.\n\n        try:\n            if not keep:\n                shutil.rmtree(home)\n        finally:\n            # Always clear the slot — even if rmtree raised, the path\n            # is no longer trustworthy for future env() / state_path()\n            # consumers and callers will re-run setup() if they want a\n            # fresh HOME.\n            object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n\n    def state_path(self, suffix: str) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Return ``home_path / suffix`` after a light containment check.\n\n        ``suffix`` must be a relative path (no leading separator) and\n        must not contain ``..`` components — otherwise the returned\n        path could escape ``home_path`` and undermine the\n        per-eval HOME isolation. Full FR-ISO2 containment\n        (symlink resolution + allowlist) lands in T02.08; this method\n        performs only the minimum check necessary to keep the\n        primitive safe in isolation.\n\n        Raises :class:`ValueError` for any suffix that escapes\n        ``home_path``. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QuS3BaYDpwJXsW3fyTR4sJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# D-0029 — FR-ISO2 Path Containment Guard\n2\t\n3\t**Task**: T02.08 (Phase 2 — cliEval harness)\n4\t**Tier**: STRICT\n5\t**Risk**: High (security-critical, defense-in-depth)\n6\t**Roadmap**: FR-ISO2 + NFR-SEC2 (loader-bypass / refusal-before-side-effects)\n7\t**Cross-links**: D-0028 (HomeIsolation method surface, T02.07), AC12 / T01.19 (resolve_scratch_root), FR-SCH2 / T01.05 (validate_eval_id)\n8\t\n9\t## Goal\n10\t\n11\tProvide a single helper, `containment_guard(home_path, scratch_root, eval_id, *, config)`, that the cliEval harness invokes AFTER `tempfile.mkdtemp` materialized a per-eval HOME and BEFORE any hook adapter writes a single byte under it. The guard fails closed: any of three independent checks raising surfaces a `HomeContainmentViolation`; the partial per-eval HOME is left on disk so the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can tag it as `setup_failed` for forensic inspection.\n12\t\n13\t## Public API\n14\t\n15\t### `HomeContainmentViolation(Exception)`\n16\t\n17\tSingle hard-failure exception type. Callers may catch the base class; branching on the underlying cause is forensics-only.\n18\t\n19\t| Field | Type | Source |\n20\t|---|---|---|\n21\t| `check` | `str` | One of `\"eval_id\"`, `\"scratch_root_allowlist\"`, `\"home_path_resolution\"`, `\"home_path_escape\"`. Lets reporters bucket failures without parsing the message. |\n22\t| `home_path` | `Path` | Verbatim input (no resolution). |\n23\t| `scratch_root` | `Path` | Verbatim input. |\n24\t| `eval_id` | `object` | Verbatim input (`repr`-rendered); non-string scalars stay visible. |\n25\t| `detail` | `str` | Human-readable one-line cause. |\n26\t\n27\t`__cause__` carries the underlying `InvalidEvalId`, `ScratchRootViolation`, or `FileNotFoundError` (chained via `raise ... from`).\n28\t\n29\t### `containment_guard(home_path, scratch_root, eval_id, *, config: EvalConfig) -> None`\n30\t\n31\tThree checks run in order. First failure short-circuits:\n32\t\n33\t1. **eval_id regex (loader-bypass defense)** — re-applies `validate_eval_id` (T01.05) so a caller that bypassed `SuiteLoader` (programmatic test, future REPL) still hard-fails. Raises `HomeContainmentViolation(check=\"eval_id\", ..., __cause__=InvalidEvalId)`.\n34\t2. **Scratch-root allowlist (AC12)** — re-routes through `resolve_scratch_root(scratch_root, config=config)`. Catches a scratch root that is a symlink to a non-allowlisted target (the helper resolves with `strict=False` so symlinks collapse). The allowlist is sourced exclusively from `config.allowed_scratch_roots`; the guard **refuses to synthesize a fallback** (NFR-SEC2 — see \"Bypass surface closed\" below). Raises `HomeContainmentViolation(check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\", ..., __cause__=ScratchRootViolation)`.\n35\t3. **Post-mkdtemp symlink-resolved containment** — `home_path.resolve(strict=True)` collapses every symlink component and forces the HOME to exist on disk; the resolved form must equal or live beneath the resolved `scratch_root`. Raises `HomeContainmentViolation(check=\"home_path_resolution\", ..., __cause__=FileNotFoundError)` when the HOME has not been materialized, or `check=\"home_path_escape\"` when the resolved form is outside `scratch_root`.\n36\t\n37\t### `HomeIsolation.setup(*, config: EvalConfig) -> Path`\n38\t\n39\tIntegrates the guard:\n40\t\n41\t1. Raises `RuntimeError` if `setup` was already called on this instance and `teardown` has not run since (idempotency rule preserved from T02.07).\n42\t2. `mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` on `self.home_root` so the scratch root exists.\n43\t3. `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f\"{eval_id}-\", dir=str(self.home_root))` mints the per-eval HOME. The path is stored on the private `_home_path` slot via `object.__setattr__`.\n44\t4. **`containment_guard(home_path=home, scratch_root=self.home_root, eval_id=self.eval_id, config=config)`** — the FR-ISO2 check runs here, AFTER the slot is populated, BEFORE return. Any failure surfaces verbatim; the slot stays populated so the NFR-ISO2 wrapper (T02.13) can find the partial HOME.\n45\t\n46\t`config` is a required keyword-only argument. No default. No fallback. See \"Bypass surface closed\".\n47\t\n48\t## Bypass surface closed (NFR-SEC2)\n49\t\n50\tA predecessor draft of `setup()` accepted `config: EvalConfig | None = None` and, when `None`, synthesized:\n51\t\n52\t```python\n53\tEvalConfig(\n54\t    allowed_scratch_roots=(self.home_root, *EvalConfig().allowed_scratch_roots),\n55\t)\n56\t```\n57\t\n58\tThe quality-engineer review flagged this as a security bypass: `HomeIsolation.__post_init__` validates `eval_id` (FR-SCH2) but does NOT validate `home_root`, so a caller could construct `HomeIsolation(eval_id=\"E1\", home_root=Path(\"/home/user/.claude\"), session_id=\"x\")` and have `setup()` quietly inject `/home/user/.claude` into the allowlist before invoking the guard. Check 2 would then pass trivially against an attacker-controlled root.\n59\t\n60\tThe fix landed in this deliverable:\n61\t\n62\t- `containment_guard`'s `config` parameter is required (no `None` default, no inline `EvalConfig()` fabrication).\n63\t- `HomeIsolation.setup`'s `config` parameter is required.\n64\t- The home_root-injection fallback is deleted.\n65\t\n66\tRefusal-before-side-effects is preserved: a caller who omits `config` triggers Python's argument-binding `TypeError` BEFORE `setup`'s body executes, so the `mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` on `self.home_root` and the `mkdtemp` call never run. Asserted by `test_setup_requires_explicit_config` (assertion: `not any(scratch_root.iterdir())` after the `TypeError`).\n67\t\n68\t## Acceptance criteria\n69\t\n70\t| AC | Source | Verified by D-0029 — FR-ISO2 Path Containment Guard\n\n**Task**: T02.08 (Phase 2 — cliEval harness)\n**Tier**: STRICT\n**Risk**: High (security-critical, defense-in-depth)\n**Roadmap**: FR-ISO2 + NFR-SEC2 (loader-bypass / refusal-before-side-effects)\n**Cross-links**: D-0028 (HomeIsolation method surface, T02.07), AC12 / T01.19 (resolve_scratch_root), FR-SCH2 / T01.05 (validate_eval_id)\n\n## Goal\n\nProvide a single helper, `containment_guard(home_path, scratch_root, eval_id, *, config)`, that the cliEval harness invokes AFTER `tempfile.mkdtemp` materialized a per-eval HOME and BEFORE any hook adapter writes a single byte under it. The guard fails closed: any of three independent checks raising surfaces a `HomeContainmentViolation`; the partial per-eval HOME is left on disk so the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can tag it as `setup_failed` for forensic inspection.\n\n## Public API\n\n### `HomeContainmentViolation(Exception)`\n\nSingle hard-failure exception type. Callers may catch the base class; branching on the underlying cause is forensics-only.\n\n| Field | Type | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| `check` | `str` | One of `\"eval_id\"`, `\"scratch_root_allowlist\"`, `\"home_path_resolution\"`, `\"home_path_escape\"`. Lets reporters bucket failures without parsing the message. |\n| `home_path` | `Path` | Verbatim input (no resolution). |\n| `scratch_root` | `Path` | Verbatim input. |\n| `eval_id` | `object` | Verbatim input (`repr`-rendered); non-string scalars stay visible. |\n| `detail` | `str` | Human-readable one-line cause. |\n\n`__cause__` carries the underlying `InvalidEvalId`, `ScratchRootViolation`, or `FileNotFoundError` (chained via `raise ... from`).\n\n### `containment_guard(home_path, scratch_root, eval_id, *, config: EvalConfig) -> None`\n\nThree checks run in order. First failure short-circuits:\n\n1. **eval_id regex (loader-bypass defense)** — re-applies `validate_eval_id` (T01.05) so a caller that bypassed `SuiteLoader` (programmatic test, future REPL) still hard-fails. Raises `HomeContainmentViolation(check=\"eval_id\", ..., __cause__=InvalidEvalId)`.\n2. **Scratch-root allowlist (AC12)** — re-routes through `resolve_scratch_root(scratch_root, config=config)`. Catches a scratch root that is a symlink to a non-allowlisted target (the helper resolves with `strict=False` so symlinks collapse). The allowlist is sourced exclusively from `config.allowed_scratch_roots`; the guard **refuses to synthesize a fallback** (NFR-SEC2 — see \"Bypass surface closed\" below). Raises `HomeContainmentViolation(check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\", ..., __cause__=ScratchRootViolation)`.\n3. **Post-mkdtemp symlink-resolved containment** — `home_path.resolve(strict=True)` collapses every symlink component and forces the HOME to exist on disk; the resolved form must equal or live beneath the resolved `scratch_root`. Raises `HomeContainmentViolation(check=\"home_path_resolution\", ..., __cause__=FileNotFoundError)` when the HOME has not been materialized, or `check=\"home_path_escape\"` when the resolved form is outside `scratch_root`.\n\n### `HomeIsolation.setup(*, config: EvalConfig) -> Path`\n\nIntegrates the guard:\n\n1. Raises `RuntimeError` if `setup` was already called on this instance and `teardown` has not run since (idempotency rule preserved from T02.07).\n2. `mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` on `self.home_root` so the scratch root exists.\n3. `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f\"{eval_id}-\", dir=str(self.home_root))` mints the per-eval HOME. The path is stored on the private `_home_path` slot via `object.__setattr__`.\n4. **`containment_guard(home_path=home, scratch_root=self.home_root, eval_id=self.eval_id, config=config)`** — the FR-ISO2 check runs here, AFTER the slot is populated, BEFORE return. Any failure surfaces verbatim; the slot stays populated so the NFR-ISO2 wrapper (T02.13) can find the partial HOME.\n\n`config` is a required keyword-only argument. No default. No fallback. See \"Bypass surface closed\".\n\n## Bypass surface closed (NFR-SEC2)\n\nA predecessor draft of `setup()` accepted `config: EvalConfig | None = None` and, when `None`, synthesized:\n\n```python\nEvalConfig(\n    allowed_scratch_roots=(self.home_root, *EvalConfig().allowed_scratch_roots),\n)\n```\n\nThe quality-engineer review flagged this as a security bypass: `HomeIsolation.__post_init__` validates `eval_id` (FR-SCH2) but does NOT validate `home_root`, so a caller could construct `HomeIsolation(eval_id=\"E1\", home_root=Path(\"/home/user/.claude\"), session_id=\"x\")` and have `setup()` quietly inject `/home/user/.claude` into the allowlist before invoking the guard. Check 2 would then pass trivially against an attacker-controlled root.\n\nThe fix landed in this deliverable:\n\n- `containment_guard`'s `config` parameter is required (no `None` default, no inline `EvalConfig()` fabrication).\n- `HomeIsolation.setup`'s `config` parameter is required.\n- The home_root-injection fallback is deleted.\n\nRefusal-before-side-effects is preserved: a caller who omits `config` triggers Python's argument-binding `TypeError` BEFORE `setup`'s body executes, so the `mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` on `self.home_root` and the `mkdtemp` call never run. Asserted by `test_setup_requires_explicit_config` (assertion: `not any(scratch_root.iterdir())` after the `TypeError`).\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n| AC | Source | Verified by |\n|---|---|---|\n| `containment_guard` raises on FR-SCH2-rejected eval_id | T02.08 AC | `TestEvalIdReValidation.test_raises_on_unsafe_eval_id` (10 parametrized cases) |\n| `containment_guard` raises on non-string eval_id | NFR-SEC2 | `TestEvalIdReValidation.test_non_string_eval_id_is_rejected` (12 parametrized cases: int, bool, None, Path, bytes, list, dict, tuple, float) |\n| `containment_guard` raises when scratch root not in allowlist | AC12 | `TestScratchRootAllowlist.test_raises_when_scratch_root_outside_default_allowlist`, `test_uses_evalconfig_allowed_scratch_roots_as_source_of_truth` |\n| `containment_guard` catches scratch root symlinked to non-allowlisted target | AC12 + symlink defense | `TestScratchRootAllowlist.test_raises_when_scratch_root_symlinked_outside_allowlist` |\n| catches home_path symlink escape | T02.08 AC | `TestSymlinkResolvedContainment.test_raises_when_home_path_symlink_escapes_scratch_root`, `test_raises_on_symlink_chain_escape` (A→B→outside) |\n| Allowlist source-of-truth = `EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots` (no hard-coded copy) | AC12 / FR-ISO2 | `TestScratchRootAllowlist.test_uses_evalconfig_allowed_scratch_roots_as_source_of_truth` |\n| OQ-4 (NOTICE/LICENSE for ptytest) must be resolved before M2 entry (before vendored sources physically land).\n\n## Milestone Summary\n\n|ID|Title|Type|Priority|Effort|Dependencies|Deliverables|Risk|\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n|M1|Foundation: Config, Schema, DSL Interface, Security Loader|infrastructure|P0|L|none|22|HIGH|\n|M2|Isolation, Process Layer, Vendored ptytest|infrastructure|P0|L|M1|22|HIGH|\n|M3|Execution Engine & Reporter|core|P0|XL|M2|19|MEDIUM|\n--\n|29|FR-ISO2|Path containment guard (security-critical)|Re-check eval_id regex; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01GXrrJP4hkoA3e3EfLuZBqG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MNfCbYyLMwvuZtRyCKUndZ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/config/workspace/IronClaude/tests/cli/eval/test_defense_in_depth.py","content":"\"\"\"NFR-SEC2 defense-in-depth attack matrix (Task T02.09 / D-0030).\n\nThis module is the *positive containment-rejection* test deliverable for\nNFR-SEC2. The unit-level FR-ISO2 surface lives in\n``tests/cli/eval/test_path_containment.py`` (T02.08 / D-0029); this\nmodule sits one layer higher and exercises :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`\nend-to-end against the four NFR-SEC2 attack vectors enumerated in the\ncliEval roadmap (row 30):\n\n    1. ``scratch-is-symlink-to-HOME`` — scratch root looks legitimate\n       but is a symlink whose resolved target is non-allowlisted\n       (catastrophic case: symlinks to the real ``$HOME``/``.claude``).\n    2. ``scratch-outside-allowlist``  — scratch root is a real\n       directory but not present in\n       :attr:`EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots`.\n    3. ``eval_id-mutation-post-construction`` — a caller force-mutates\n       the (frozen) ``eval_id`` field after\n       ``HomeIsolation.__post_init__`` ran, simulating a tampered\n       instance reaching ``setup()``.\n    4. ``loader-bypass`` — a caller constructs ``HomeIsolation``\n       directly (skipping :class:`SuiteLoader`) with a clearly\n       loader-rejected id; the second-layer guard in\n       :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` (and the constructor's\n       ``__post_init__``) must still hard-fail.\n\nEach test asserts :class:`HomeContainmentViolation` (the canonical\nNFR-SEC2 failure surface) and pins the ``check`` identifier so\nreporters can bucket each vector without parsing the message.\n``__cause__`` is asserted where applicable to verify the underlying\nexception is chained for forensic walks.\n\nCross-links:\n\n* NFR-SEC2 roadmap row 30 (``allowed_scratch_roots`` allowlist +\n  loader-bypass defense + post-construction tamper defense).\n* FR-ISO2 unit surface (T02.08): ``tests/cli/eval/test_path_containment.py``.\n* NFR-SEC3 real-HOME hard guard (T02.10):\n  ``tests/cli/eval/test_hard_guard_real_home.py``.\n* Allowlist source-of-truth (AC12 / T01.19):\n  ``tests/cli/eval/test_scratch_root_allowlist.py``.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom superclaude.cli.eval import (\n    EvalConfig,\n    HomeContainmentViolation,\n    HomeIsolation,\n    InvalidEvalId,\n    ScratchRootViolation,\n)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Fixtures\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef scratch_root(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Per-test scratch root mirroring the orchestrator's ``--scratch-root``.\n\n    The directory exists on disk so :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` can\n    legitimately ``mkdtemp`` under it for any test whose attack vector\n    isn't the scratch root itself.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    root = tmp_path / \"eval-runs\"\n    root.mkdir()\n    return root\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef permissive_config(scratch_root: Path) -> EvalConfig:\n    \"\"\"``EvalConfig`` whose allowlist explicitly includes the test's scratch\n    root.\n\n    Mirrors production wiring: the orchestrator (T03.16) hands the\n    runner a config whose ``allowed_scratch_roots`` already contains\n    the resolved scratch root for the run. Tests that target a vector\n    *other* than the allowlist need a permissive config so the failure\n    that surfaces is the vector under test (not a coincidental\n    allowlist miss).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    return EvalConfig(allowed_scratch_roots=(scratch_root,))\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Attack vector 1 — scratch root is a symlink to a non-allowlisted target\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestVectorScratchSymlinkToHome:\n    \"\"\"Vector 1 (``scratch-is-symlink-to-HOME``).\n\n    The catastrophic case from the roadmap risk register (R2): a\n    scratch root that *looks* like an allowed prefix but is actually a\n    symlink whose resolved target lies outside the allowlist — the\n    real ``$HOME``/``.claude`` being the worst-case target. The\n    AC12 allowlist check inside :func:`containment_guard` calls\n    :func:`resolve_scratch_root`, which resolves with\n    ``strict=False`` so any symlink component collapses before the\n    membership test runs. The expected failure surface is\n    :class:`HomeContainmentViolation` with\n    ``check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\"`` and ``__cause__`` chained to\n    :class:`ScratchRootViolation`.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def test_setup_rejects_scratch_root_symlinked_outside_allowlist(\n        self, tmp_path: Path\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Build a scratch root whose textual path is allowlist-friendly\n        but whose symlink target points at a stand-in for the real\n        ``$HOME`` (a directory deliberately outside the allowlist).\n\n        Calling :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` MUST raise\n        :class:`HomeContainmentViolation` with\n        ``check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\"`` and a chained\n        :class:`ScratchRootViolation` cause — proving the\n        symlink-resolved allowlist check is the layer that catches the\n        attack rather than any lexical prefix comparison.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Stand-in for the real ``$HOME``/``.claude`` an attacker would\n        # try to escape into. Deliberately *not* in the allowlist.\n        fake_real_home = tmp_path / \"FAKE_HOME_DOT_CLAUDE\"\n        fake_real_home.mkdir()\n        (fake_real_home / \"settings.json\").write_text(\"{}\")  # plausible target\n\n        # The \"legitimate-looking\" scratch root the caller passes is a\n        # symlink to the fake real HOME. Its lexical parent path is\n        # under tmp_path, but ``Path.resolve(strict=False)`` will\n        # collapse it to ``fake_real_home``.\n        scratch_link = tmp_path / \"looks-like-eval-runs\"\n        scratch_link.symlink_to(fake_real_home)\n\n        # Real allowlisted directory the operator *intended* — note it\n        # is a different, real directory; the symlink target is NOT\n        # this path.\n        allowed_dir = tmp_path / \"really-allowed\"\n        allowed_dir.mkdir()\n        config = EvalConfig(allowed_scratch_roots=(allowed_dir,))\n\n        iso = HomeIsolation(\n            eval_id=\"E1\",\n            home_root=scratch_link,\n            session_id=\"sess-vector-1\",\n        )\n\n        with pytest.raises(HomeContainmentViolation) as exc_info:\n            iso.setup(config=config)\n\n        assert exc_info.value.check == \"scratch_root_allowlist\"\n        assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, ScratchRootViolation)\n        # The exception payload preserves the verbatim (un-resolved)\n        # scratch root so forensics can show the operator the symlink\n        # they were tricked into supplying.\n        assert exc_info.value.scratch_root == scratch_link\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Attack vector 2 — scratch root outside the AC12 allowlist\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestVectorScratchOutsideAllowlist:\n    \"\"\"Vector 2 (``scratch-outside-allowlist``).\n\n    The scratch root is a real, non-symlinked directory whose path is\n    not present in :attr:`EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots`. This is\n    the \"policy\" failure mode that catches a configuration mistake or\n    a malicious ``--scratch-root`` flag value pointing at an\n    unintended location.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def test_setup_rejects_scratch_root_not_in_allowlist(\n        self, tmp_path: Path\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"A real scratch directory that is not listed in the config's\n        ``allowed_scratch_roots`` must fail with ``check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\"``.\"\"\"\n\n        # Real directory, not symlinked anywhere.\n        scratch = tmp_path / \"off-policy-scratch\"\n        scratch.mkdir()\n\n        # Allowlist contains a *different* directory, mirroring a real\n        # production config (e.g. only ``/tmp/eval-runs`` is permitted).\n        allowed_only = tmp_path / \"only-allowed-policy\"\n        allowed_only.mkdir()\n        config = EvalConfig(allowed_scratch_roots=(allowed_only,))\n\n        iso = HomeIsolation(\n            eval_id=\"E1\",\n            home_root=scratch,\n            session_id=\"sess-vector-2\",\n        )\n\n        with pytest.raises(HomeContainmentViolation) as exc_info:\n            iso.setup(config=config)\n\n        assert exc_info.value.check == \"scratch_root_allowlist\"\n        assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, ScratchRootViolation)\n        assert exc_info.value.scratch_root == scratch\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Attack vector 3 — eval_id mutation after construction\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestVectorEvalIdMutationPostConstruction:\n    \"\"\"Vector 3 (``eval_id-mutation-post-construction``).\n\n    :class:`HomeIsolation` is a ``@dataclass(frozen=True)``; ordinary\n    assignment raises :class:`dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError`. However\n    a determined caller can still bypass the freeze via\n    ``object.__setattr__`` (the same mechanism the class itself uses\n    for the private ``_home_path`` slot). The defense-in-depth contract\n    requires that even an instance whose ``eval_id`` was force-mutated\n    *after* ``__post_init__`` ran still fails inside\n    :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` — the guard re-runs\n    :func:`validate_eval_id` so post-construction tampering cannot\n    sneak an unsafe id into ``tempfile.mkdtemp``.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    @pytest.mark.parametrize(\n        \"tampered_eval_id\",\n        [\n            \"../escape\",  # path traversal\n            \"/etc/passwd\",  # absolute path injection\n            \"E1/x\",  # path separator smuggled in\n            \"..\",  # parent-directory token\n            \"9bad\",  # FR-SCH2 leading-digit reject\n            \"\",  # empty string\n            \"with spaces\",\n            \"E1\\nE2\",  # newline injection\n        ],\n    )\n    def test_setup_rejects_post_construction_eval_id_mutation(\n        self,\n        scratch_root: Path,\n        permissive_config: EvalConfig,\n        tampered_eval_id: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Construct ``HomeIsolation`` with a valid id (passes\n        ``__post_init__``), then force-mutate ``eval_id`` to a FR-SCH2-\n        rejected value via ``object.__setattr__`` and call\n        :meth:`setup`. The containment guard's eval_id check must\n        surface a :class:`HomeContainmentViolation` with\n        ``check=\"eval_id\"`` and :class:`InvalidEvalId` chained as\n        ``__cause__``.\"\"\"\n\n        iso = HomeIsolation(\n            eval_id=\"E1\",  # safe at construction\n            home_root=scratch_root,\n            session_id=\"sess-vector-3\",\n        )\n\n        # Bypass the dataclass freeze the same way an attacker (or an\n        # accidental ``__setattr__`` shim) would.\n        object.__setattr__(iso, \"eval_id\", tampered_eval_id)\n\n        with pytest.raises(HomeContainmentViolation) as exc_info:\n            iso.setup(config=permissive_config)\n\n        assert exc_info.value.check == \"eval_id\"\n        assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, InvalidEvalId)\n        assert exc_info.value.eval_id == tampered_eval_id\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Attack vector 4 — loader-bypass: caller skips SuiteLoader entirely\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestVectorLoaderBypass:\n    \"\"\"Vector 4 (``loader-bypass``).\n\n    The FR-SCH2 regex enforcement lives in\n    :func:`validate_eval_id` and is applied by\n    :class:`SuiteLoader` on every parsed-and-expanded eval id (T01.05\n    + T01.07). NFR-SEC2 mandates that a caller skipping the loader —\n    for example a programmatic test, a future REPL entry point, or an\n    accidentally instantiated ``HomeIsolation`` in an integration\n    fixture — STILL fails closed. Two layers enforce this:\n\n    1. ``HomeIsolation.__post_init__`` re-runs\n       :func:`validate_eval_id` at construction.\n    2. :func:`containment_guard` re-runs\n       :func:`validate_eval_id` inside :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`.\n\n    The first layer raises :class:`InvalidEvalId` directly because\n    ``__post_init__`` runs before any guard-wrapping; the second is\n    proven separately by the post-construction mutation vector above.\n    Acceptance criteria require this test to prove that *constructing*\n    ``HomeIsolation`` without going through :class:`SuiteLoader` still\n    fails containment — so we assert the constructor-time defense\n    here. :class:`InvalidEvalId` is the canonical loader-bypass\n    failure surface; in production code the CLI boundary maps it to\n    :data:`INVALID_EVAL_ID_EXIT_CODE` (= 2). This pins the same\n    error class :func:`containment_guard` would have chained as\n    ``__cause__`` if the constructor's guard had been removed —\n    keeping the bypass closure consistent across both layers.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    @pytest.mark.parametrize(\n        \"loader_rejected_eval_id\",\n        [\n            \"../escape\",\n            \"/absolute/path\",\n            \"E1/with/sep\",\n            \"9bad\",\n            \"{{template}}\",\n            \"${shell}\",\n            \"E1\\nE2\",\n        ],\n    )\n    def test_construction_rejects_loader_rejected_eval_id(\n        self,\n        scratch_root: Path,\n        loader_rejected_eval_id: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Constructing :class:`HomeIsolation` directly with a\n        loader-rejected eval id must raise :class:`InvalidEvalId`\n        before any filesystem operation becomes reachable — proving\n        the loader-bypass surface is closed at construction time.\"\"\"\n\n        with pytest.raises(InvalidEvalId) as exc_info:\n            HomeIsolation(\n                eval_id=loader_rejected_eval_id,\n                home_root=scratch_root,\n ``__post_init__`` regex check were *removed* by a refactor\n        (simulated by monkeypatching :func:`validate_eval_id` to a\n        no-op at construction time), the guard inside\n
